The Knight on d5 is currently under attack by the Black pawn on e6. I need to move it to a safe square. Let's analyze all the possible retreats for the Knight:
- d5e7+ is guarded by the Black Rook on f7 moving to e7.
- d5c7 is also guarded by the Black Rook on f7 sliding along the 7th rank.

- d5b4 allows the pawn on c5 to capture c5xb4.
- d5f4 and d5e3 both place the Knight on the dark-square diagonal controlled by the Black Bishop on g5 (g5-f4-e3). 

This leaves `d5c3` as the only safe square for the Knight to retreat to, securely removing it from the impending capture.
